- probe: "HW-PROBE-a-session-renames-the-identifier-index-of-the-graph-crate-and-its-callers"
  session: "L2-additive-scenario-2-p14-r2"
  calls:
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"find /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p14-r2 -maxdepth 6 -iname \\\"*graph*\\\" -type d 2>/dev/null\",\"description\":\"Find graph-related directories\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"find /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p14-r2/engine/crates/graph -iname \\\"*index*\\\"\",\"description\":\"Find index module files in graph crate\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"echo \\\"--- lib.rs / mod declarations ---\\\" && grep -rn \\\"mod index\\\" engine/crates/graph/src && echo \\\"--- all 'index' refs in graph crate src ---\\\" && grep -rln \\\"index\\\" engine/crates/graph/src\",\"description\":\"Search for module declaration and references to index\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"grep -rln \\\"graph::index\\\\|crate::index\\\\|use.*index\\\\b\\\" --include=\\\"*.rs\\\" . | sort\",\"description\":\"Find all Rust files referencing the index module across workspace\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"echo \\\"=== graph::index usages ===\\\" && grep -rn \\\"graph::index\\\" --include=\\\"*.rs\\\" . \\necho \\\"=== crate::index within graph crate ===\\\" && grep -rn \\\"crate::index\\\\|use.*index::\\\\|index::\\\" --include=\\\"*.rs\\\" engine/crates/graph\\necho \\\"=== graph crate Cargo.toml name ===\\\" && grep -n \\\"^name\\\" engine/crates/graph/Cargo.toml\",\"description\":\"Find precise references to graph's index module\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"cd engine/crates\\necho \\\"=== generate/src/derived.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" generate/src/derived.rs\\necho \\\"=== generate/src/shelf_sections.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" generate/src/shelf_sections.rs\\necho \\\"=== generate/src/site.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" generate/src/site.rs\\necho \\\"=== generate/src/verb_index.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" generate/src/verb_index.rs | head -20\\necho \\\"=== generate/tests/fixtures.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" generate/tests/fixtures.rs\\necho \\\"=== generate/tests/shelf_index_filter.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" generate/tests/shelf_index_filter.rs | head -20\\necho \\\"=== compat/src/lib.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" compat/src/lib.rs\\necho \\\"=== probe/src/grade.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" probe/src/grade.rs\\necho \\\"=== query/src/route.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" query/src/route.rs\\necho \\\"=== verbs/src/lib.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" verbs/src/lib.rs\\necho \\\"=== cli/tests/publish_order.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" cli/tests/publish_order.rs\\necho \\\"=== graph/src/declarations.rs ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" graph/src/declarations.rs\\necho \\\"=== sweep/tests/fixtures.rs other mentions ===\\\" ; grep -n \\\"index\\\" sweep/tests/fixtures.rs\\n\",\"description\":\"Check context of index references in various crates\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"cd graph/src\\necho \\\"=== struct field named index in graph crate ===\\\" \\ngrep -rn \\\"pub index\\\\|    index:\\\" . \\necho \\\"=== lib.rs context around pub use index ===\\\" \\nsed -n '55,75p;125,140p' lib.rs\\necho \\\"=== declarations.rs context ===\\\" \\nsed -n '1,20p' declarations.rs\\necho \\\"=== anchors.rs context ===\\\" \\nsed -n '30,45p;730,745p' anchors.rs\\necho \\\"=== links.rs context ===\\\"\\nsed -n '55,70p' links.rs\\necho \\\"=== edges.rs context ===\\\"\\nsed -n '45,60p' edges.rs\",\"description\":\"Inspect struct fields and context around index module usage in graph crate\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"cd /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p14-r2\\necho \\\"=== docs referencing graph index module ===\\\" \\ngrep -rln \\\"headwater_graph::index\\\\|graph::index\\\\|headwater-graph/index\\\\|graph/src/index\\\" docs 2>/dev/null\\necho \\\"=== compat/src/lib.rs full relevant context ===\\\" \\nsed -n '1,20p;295,310p;645,660p' engine/crates/compat/src/lib.rs\\necho \\\"=== any other 'index' backtick-only refs near graph crate docs (possible rustdoc intra-links) ===\\\"\\ngrep -rn '\\\\[`index\\\\b\\\\|\\\\[`crate::index\\\\b\\\\|\\\\[index\\\\]\\\\|\\\\[crate::index\\\\]' engine/crates --include=\\\"*.rs\\\"\\n\",\"description\":\"Check docs and compat crate for index references\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"cd /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p14-r2\\ngrep -n \\\"index\\\" docs/decisions/0068-the-front-matter-key-that-carries-a-minted-identifier-is-id.md\",\"description\":\"Check docs decision file for index references\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Read"

      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"grep -n \\\"patterns\\\" /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p3-r1/engine/crates/generate/tests/fixtures.rs\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Read"
      argument: "/mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p3-r1/engine/crates/generate/tests/fixtures.rs"
      result: "sha256:a0a790fe108e07e65fdd384897b1d84580b7a9ffa2481d7f7ab3980ed7c8d19d"
    - tool: "Read"
      argument: "/mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p3-r1/docs/interfaces/headwater-export.md"
      result: "sha256:b9da421ea91d4647cc53fe44c2062ad139aaff570034baa83b32ad551fd3f526"
    - tool: "Grep"
      argument: "/mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p3-r1"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Read"
      argument: "/mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p3-r1/docs/acceptance-criteria/0004-each-list-anchor-in-the-native-export-carries-its-sorted-members-and-a-single-pattern-carries-none.md"
      result: "sha256:55b7ed864802312fcfa54e0b35714063d27783616094b2b7ca83adbadd740a49"
    - tool: "Agent"
      argument: "{\"description\":\"Survey doc-governance ties for list-anchor patterns behavior\",\"prompt\":\"This is a Rust repo at /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p3-r1 called \\\"headwater\\\" that uses a heavy doc-governance system: docs/acceptance-criteria/, docs/requirements/, docs/verifications/, docs/decisions/, each with an ID (HW-AC-####, HW-REQ-####, HW-VER-####, HW-DR-####) and YAML frontmatter with `relations` cross-links, plus a `.headwater/ids/` directory indexing IDs.\\n\\nI am about to change behavior in engine/crates/generate/src/export.rs: currently the `list_members` function (and the native/schema emitters) write a `patterns` member on a list anchor (and on an edge target bound to an anchor) ONLY when the anchor holds more than one pattern. I am changing it so `patterns` is written for EVERY anchor, always, and for every edge onto an anchor, always.\\n\\nThis directly contradicts:\\n- docs/acceptance-criteria/0004-each-list-anchor-in-the-native-export-carries-its-sorted-members-and-a-single-pattern-carries-none.md (HW-AC-0004)\\n- docs/requirements/0004-the-graph-export-carries-the-member-patterns-of-a-list-anchor.md (HW-REQ-0004)\\n- docs/verifications/0003-a-fixture-tree-of-three-list-anchors-proves-that-the-export-carries-list-members.md (HW-VER-0003)\\n- the test `the_native_export_carries_the_members_of_a_list_anchor` in engine/crates/generate/tests/fixtures.rs, which asserts the single-pattern anchor `src/c.rs` has NO `patterns` member.\\n- docs/interfaces/headwater-export.md table rows and the \\\"Raising the version\\\" history section (version 1.3).\\n\\nI need to know, so I can update everything consistently without missing a cross-check that would fail CI:\\n\\n1. Is there any automated test/lint that checks consistency between docs/acceptance-criteria, docs/requirements, docs/verifications files and their relations (e.g. that an AC's fit criterion text matches what a test actually checks, or that ID files form a valid graph)? Look for something like a \\\"docs consistency\\\" test, an ID registry validator, or a script under tools/ or .headwater/ that walks these files.\\n2. Does `.headwater/ids/acceptance_criterion_id/HW-AC-0004` or similar need manual updating (is it just a file containing the ID, or does it get regenerated by a command)?\\n3. Are there other files anywhere in the repo (docs, fixtures, nav.yml, etc.) that reference HW-AC-0004, HW-REQ-0004, or HW-VER-0003, or that quote the phrase \\\"a single pattern carries none\\\" / \\\"single-pattern anchor\\\", that would become stale?\\n4. Context  \\\", \\\"context\\\"),\\n    ];\\n    for (input, expected) in cases {\\n        let got = normalized(input);\\n        let ok = got == expected;\\n        println!(\\\"{ok} {input:?} -> {got:?} (expected {expected:?})\\\");\\n    }\\n}\\nEOF\\nrustc -O /tmp/test_unnumbered.rs -o /tmp/test_unnumbered 2>&1 && /tmp/test_unnumbered\"}"

  calls:
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"ls -la /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p5-r2\"}"

      result: ""
    - tool: "Agent"
      argument: "{\"description\":\"Research headwater obligation/governs/discharge semantics\",\"subagent_type\":\"Explore\",\"prompt\":\"I'm working in the repo at /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L2-additive-scenario-2-p5-r2. This is a \\\"Headwater\\\" documentation/traceability system (see the .headwater/ directory with taxonomy.yml, overlay.yml, nav.yml, probe.yml, and .headwater/ids/ subdirectories holding IDs like HW-OBL-xxxx (obligation_record_id), HW-REQ-xxxx (requirement_id), HW-SPEC-xxx (spec_id), HW-PD-xxxx (process_decision_id), HW-AC-xxxx (acceptance_criterion_id), HW-VER-xxxx (verification_id), HW-REG-xxx (register_id), HW-LIB-xxx (library_doctrine_id)).\\n\\nI need to understand this corpus's specific domain model/ontology well enough to carry out a task. The task mentions these specific concepts which I need you to find definitions and conventions for in the docs/specs:\\n\\n1. An \\\"obligation\\\" record that is \\\"recorded\\\" when two relations/edges \\\"name a hook as their author\\\" and \\\"no hook writes them\\\" — i.e., some kind of integrity/consistency obligation about hooks and relations/edges in this system.\\n2. \\\"governs\\\" edges — a specific edge/relation type in this taxonomy.\\n3. The idea that \\\"the evaluation of governs edges\\\" can mark an obligation as \\\"discharged\\\".\\n4. The convention this corpus uses to \\\"link two documents\\\" when recording that an obligation has been discharged — i.e., is there a specific frontmatter field, relation type, or document linking convention (e.g. \\\"discharges:\\\", \\\"resolves:\\\", a specific relation edge type like \\\"discharged_by\\\" or similar) used to link a discharge record/decision document to the obligation document it discharges?\\n\\nPlease look at:\\n- .headwater/taxonomy.yml, .headwater/overlay.yml, .headwater/nav.yml, .headwater/probe.yml for schema/edge type definitions (especially anything about \\\"governs\\\", \\\"hook\\\", \\\"obligation\\\", \\\"discharge\\\", \\\"author\\\")\\n- docs/ directory for any spec or doctrine documents about obligations, the recorder contract, evidence-and-obligation doctrine (there's a HW-LIB-evidence-and-obligation id), decision records (HW-LIB-decision-record), and relations/edges model (HW-SPEC-conceptual-model, HW-SPEC-taxonomy-model)\\n- Any existing obligation_record documents (search docs/ or elsewhere for files with HW-OBL- ids, especially ones that are already marked discharged, to see the actual linking pattern/frontmatter used)\\n- Any existing example of two documents linked via a \\\"discharge\\\" relationship, to see the exact field names and syntax\\n\\nReport back with:\\n- The exact schema/field names for obligation records and how they reference what they discharge (or what discharges them)\\n- What a \\\"hook\\\" is in this system and what \\\"governs\\\" edges mean\\n- A concrete example (quote frontmatter/YAML) of an existing discharged obligation linked to its discharging document, if one exists\\n- The file paths of the most relevant spec/doctrine documents\\n\\nKeep the report focused and include exact quotes/field names, under 500 words plus necessary quotes.\"}"
      result: ""
